2b2(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin dang dbang dang /mngon par shes pa dang ma 'dres pa dang /byang chub kyi yan lag dang /lam dang sa bcu dang /pha rol tu phyin pa dang /thabs la mkhas pa dang /bsdu ba'i dngos po dang /byams pa dang 
2b3(rgyud): snying rje dang /dga' ba dang btang snyoms dang /byams pa'i stobs dang dben pa dang /yongs su byang ba'i sems kyi rgyun dang ldan pa sha stag la 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje 
2b4(rgyud): snying po zhes bya ba dang /rdo rje mig dang rdo rje'i lus dang /rdo rje'i blo gros dang lag na rdo rje dang /rdo rje 'dus pa dang rdo rje mthu bo che dang /rdo rje rnam par 'phrul pa dang /rdo rje brtsegs pa dang /rdo rje'i sha po dang /
2b5(rgyud): rdo rje'i nga ro dang rdo rje bzang po dang /byang chub sems dpa' sesams dpa' chen po rdo rje'i tog ces bya ba dang /de dag la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag brgyad 
2b6(rgyud): cu rtsa bzhi dang /nyan thos chen po rab tu mang po thams cad kyang dgra bcom pa//zag pa zad pa/srid par kun tu sbyor ba chad pa/yang dag pa'i shes pas sems shin du rnam par grol ba/rdzu 'phrul gyi

2b8(rgyud): sregs pa sha stag la 'di lta ste/tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu dang /tshe dang ldan pa byams ma'i bu gang po dang /tshe dang ldan pa ka ba ni dang /tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor dang /tshe dang ldan pa mau dgal gyi bu chen po dang /

3b1(rgyud): nga rgya'i g.yog can dang /'jig rten gyi ma bdun dang /sring mo bdun dang /drang srong chen po bdun dang /bar snang la rgyu ba'i rgyu skar dang /gza'i lha thams cad dang /phyogs kyi lha rnams dang /phyogs mtshams kyi lha

3b5(rgyud): dang lag 'dren gyi dbang po bgegs log 'dren gyi g.yog du ma can dang /shing gseb kyi lha drug cu dang /sring mo bzhi ming po dang bcas pa dang /ma bdun dang rdo rje lcags kyu can dang /rdo rje lu gu rgyud dang /rdo rje po nya 
3b6(rgyud): mo drug cu rtsa bzhi dang /rdo rje'i sde dang /lag bzangs dang gtsug gis 'gro rdo rje'i rigs kyi 'khor du ma dang bcas pa dang /de ma yin pa gzhan yang sangs rgyas dang /chos dang /dge 'dun la mngon par dad pa'i 
3b7(rgyud): lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi'am ci dang lto 'phye chen po dang /'byung po dang yi dgas dang /sha za dang smyo byed dang /brjed byed dang /sgrub pa dang /sbrul 'dzin

4a1(rgyud): @#//thabs gcig go/de ltar yang bcom ldan 'das de chos kyi 'khor lo ni shin du bskor sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa ni shin tu yongs su mthar phyin/bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs ni shin tu yongs 
4a2(rgyud): su rdzogs thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dang /byang chub chen po dang /pha rol tu phyin pa dang /sa brnyes pas ni shin du yongs su gzungs skyes bu chen po'i mtshan 'bar ba sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis ni sku brgyan/dpe byad bzang po

7a1(rgyud): @#//kye ma mgon po khyod kyis bshad//'khor lo yan lag bcu gnyis pa//khyod nyid mkhyen nas rab tu bskor//mu stegs can dung bdud dngul te//chos kyi mang bas thal bar mdzad//nyon mongs brag chen 
7a2(rgyud): rnam kun tu//ye shes rdo rjes rab bcom nas//sus kyang thob par ma gyur pa'i//byang chub bla na med pa brnyes//gang gi mtshan yang rdzogs pa yi//byang chub pha rol nyid 'gyur ba//khyod ni 'jig rten mchog gcig pu//dam pa 
7a3(rgyud): rang sbyin bdag po lags//khyod ni mgon po khyod ni pha//khyod skyabs khyod ni dpung gnyen lags//thugs rje chen po la shes nyid//mya ngan 'das nas gcig pa khyod//khyod nyid gshegs nas sems can rnams//
7a4(rgyud): sems can dmyal las rnam par grol//rang gi sku yang spongs btang nas//khyod kyi srog chags rnams kyang bsrungs//lus chad kun gyi phal thob yig bsregs khyod kyis brnyes gyur nas//grol te 'jig rten 
7a5(rgyud): rnams kyang 'gol//kye ma khyod ni rtsa lag bzang //mgon po khyod kyi 'od zer gyi//dra bas 'jig rten khams rnams khyab//mya ngan 'das las lhag pa bde//lus can kun gyis thob par mdzad//khyod kyi 
7a6(rgyud): 'od kyis gzis bcom pas//gza' rnams kun dang dgung zla dang //gdugs rnams dang ni zhugs rnams dang //nor bu lha mer ma gyur to//de nas gsang ba pa'i bdag po la gan rdo rjes tshul dang 'dra ba'i 
7a7(rgyud): tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis/bcom ldan 'das la mngon par bstod nas/yang rin po che'i padama'i snying po la 'dug ste/bcom ldan 'das legs so zhes gsol nas/rdo rje'i rnam par thar pa'i mgo chen po'i ting 
7a8(rgyud): nge 'dzin bdud kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad 'joms par byed pa zhes bya ba la snyoms par zhugs so//gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rjes rdo rje'i rnam par thar pa'i sgo chen po'i ting nge 'dzin bdud kyi dkyil 
7b1(rgyud): 'khor thams cad 'joms par byed pa zhes bya ba 'di la snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu/de nas de'i tshe stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di rnam pa drug tu g.yos/kun tu btu g.yos te/shar phyogs 
7b2(rgyud): mthon nub phyogs dma'/nub phyogs mthon shar phyogs dma'/lho phyogs mthon bya ba phyogs dma'/byang phyogs mthon lho phyogs dma'/mkha' mthon dbus dma'/dbus mthon ni mtha' dma' bar gyur to//phyogs 
7b3(rgyud): thams cad dang phyogs mtshams bcas cad kyang dga' ba dang /yid bde ba skyes par gyur nas/lham ma/lhan ne/lhang nge'o//gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rje'i yan lag dang nying lag gi mtshams thams cad nas rdo rje 
7b4(rgyud): kun tu 'phar ba/rab tu 'bar ba kun du rab tu mched cing 'bar ba me lce gcig tu gyur pa dag byung bar kun tu snang ngo//rdo rje 'bar ba'i snang ba des phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams tshad med grangs med dpag tu med pa ji snyed pa 
7b5(rgyud): dag na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das bar dag da ltar bzhugs te 'tsho zhing bzhes la chos kyang ston te/tshangs par spod pa thog mar dge ba bar du dge ba tha mar dge ba/don bzang po tshig 'bru bzang po/ma 'des pa yongs su rdzogs 
7b6(rgyud): pa/yongs su dag pa/yongs su byang ba/yang dag par ston pa/nyan thos kyi dge 'dun dang bcas pa/byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun dang bcas pa de dag 'khor gyi khyams rgya che zhing rgya che ba dag kun tu sna bo/byang chub sems dpa' 
7b7(rgyud): de dag thams cad kyang de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gi spyan snga na/rdo rje 'bar ba'i snang ba rgya chen po des snang bar gyur nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/de dag la so sor zhus pa/bcom ldan 'das snang ba rgya chen po kun tu gda' ba 
7b8(rgyud): 'di su'i lags/de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas de dag gis so so'i byang chub sems dpa' de dag la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu dag de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
8a1(rgyud): @#//sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa zhes bya ba ni mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams na ri rab kyi steng na chos ston te/bcom ldan 'das des bdud sdig can zhags pa'i bcing ba lngas bcings te/bcom ldan 'das 
8a2(rgyud): kyi spyan sngar 'gyel ba gzigs nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rje zhes bya ba la 'di skad du/gsang ba pa'i bdag po la ga na rdo rje khyod kyis rang gi rdzu 'phrul gyis rnam 
8a3(rgyud): par 'phrul ba'i cho 'phrul ston cig ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu dag 'di ni rdo rje'i rnam par thar pa'i sgo chen po'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/de'i rdo rje'i rnam par 'phrul pa chen po'i byin gyi rlabs so//de skad ces 
8a4(rgyud): bka' stsal te/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de dag gis gnang nas/rigs kyi bu de dag thams cad kyis de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcam pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs 
8a5(rgyud): rgyas de dag la phyag 'tshal te/byang chub sems dpa' semasad pa' chen po'i rnam par 'phrul pa'i cho 'phrul bsam gyis mi khyab pas/'jig rten gyi khams mi mjed 'dir lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs 
8a6(rgyud): pa dgra bcom pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa dang gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rje la mchod pa'i sprin dang /gdugs kyi sprin dang /rgyan phreng gi sprin dang /dgu rgyan gyi sprin dang /me tog gi sprin dang /
8a7(rgyud): na ba pa'i sprin chen pos mchod pa chen po byas nas/nam mkha'i dkyil na 'khod bzhin du bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la len bdun bskor ba byas te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga rin po che'i pad+ma'i rdo rje'i stan la btsugs 
8a8(rgyud): nas/bcom ldan 'das la logs su blta zhing /bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pas gnang nas/rang rang gi rin po che'i pad+ma'i rdo rje stan de dag la 'khod par gyur to//de'i tshe shar phyogs 
8b1(rgyud): logs na 'jig rten gyi khams rdo rje'i tog ces bya ba de na/de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdo rje'i snying pos rab tu 'joms pa zhes bya ba da ltar bzhugs te 'tsho zhing bzhes te/'di 
8b2(rgyud): ltar rdo rje'i ri rab chen po'i rtse mo'i khang pa brtsegs pa'i gzungs 'di nyid las brtsams te chos kyang ston to//rdo rje 'bar ba'i snang ba des kyang 'jig rten gyi khams ji snyed pa de dag thams cad kun nas snang bar gyur to//
8b3(rgyud): de nas 'jig rten gyi khams de na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje'i snying po zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa de'i 'khor gyi khyams su 'dus te 'dug par gyur pa des ngo mtshar de mthong nas/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 
8b4(rgyud): bcom pa yang dag par rdzog pa'i sangs rgyas rdo rje'i nying pos rab tu 'joms ba zhes bya ba de la zhus pa/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'bar ba'i snang ba 'di lta bu gang gis 'jig rten gyi khams 'di snang bar gyur te/sems 
8b5(rgyud): can thams cad kyi sdug bsngal thams cad rgyun chad cing sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro'i skye gnas dang yi dgas dang /gshin rje'i 'jig rten gyi sdug bsngal thams cad mi gda' bar gyur/lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang 
8b6(rgyud): dri za dang /lha ma yin dang nam mkha' lding dang /mi'am ci dang lto 'phye chen po la sogs pa 'jig rten na rdzu 'phrul che zhing dbang che bar grags pa thams cad kyang kun nas mchod pa'i sprin chen pos dga' ba dang yid bde ba skyes 
8b7(rgyud): te/nub phyogs su mchi bar gda' ba 'di su yi lags/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdo rje'i snying pos rab tu 'joms pas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje'i snying po 
8b8(rgyud): de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu nub phyogs kyi logs na mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra pa tsam pa yang dag par rdzogasapa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa zhes bya ba rdo rje chen po'i ri rab 
9a1(rgyud): @#//kyi rtse mo'i khang pa brtsegs pa na rdo rje chen po'i ri rab kyi rtse mo'i khang pa brtsegs ba'i gzungs shes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs ston to//bcom ldan 'das des skyil dug gi mtshams kyi mdzod spu nas 'od

9a3(rgyud): chen po dang lhan cig tu bcom ldan 'das de la gtse ba'i phyir lhags pa dang /de yang sangs rgyas kyi mthus de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdo rje'i 'khor gyi khyams de nyid du zhags pa lnga'i bcing bas bcings pas bcom ldan 'das 
9a4(rgyud): kyi spyan sngar 'gyel te/de la thugs brtse ba'i phyir bcom ldan 'das des byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rje la stan cig par bka' lung byin nas gsang ba pa'i bdag po

9a6(rgyud): snang ba'o//rigs kyi bu rdo rje 'bar ba'i snang ba des kyang phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams tshad med grangs med dpag tu med pa rnams su 'jig rten gyi khams gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma dang mnyam pa ji snyed pa snang bar byas te/
9a7(rgyud): 'jig rten gyi khams de dag na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang dag bzhugs pa de dag thams cad kyang bskul/byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor de dag kyang bskul bas ngo mtshar thob par gyur pa 
9a8(rgyud): de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa bkra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de dag la yang so sor zhus so//rigs kyi bu de nyid kyi mthus/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzobs 
9b1(rgyud): pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa de'i spyan snga na 'dug cing bsnyen bkur byed pa yang mthong ngo//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdo rje'i snying pos rab tu 'joms

9b4(rgyud): bcom pa yang dag par rdzog pa'i sangs rgyas rdo rje ri snying pos rab tu 'jom pas byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po rdo rje'i snyid po de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu de'i dus la bab par

10a1(rgyud): @#//ka dang shel dang dngul dang /nor bu dang mu tig dang /gser dang padam rA ga dang /rdo'i snying po dang /ke ke ru dang /ke ke ru chen po dang /an+da rnyil dang /mthon ka chen po dang /dun las dang /go mu ta ra ka dang /mu tig dmar po'i kha

10b3(rgyud): byung ba dag go/snying po ni dun las kyi rgyu las byang ba'o//za ba ni gser gyi rgyu las byang ba'o//chu ba ni an+da rnyil gyi rgyu la byung ba'o//ze 'bru ni//de 'bru ni dngul gyi rgyu las byung ba'o/ke ke ru'i rgyu las byung pa'i pad+ma rnams kyi 'dab ma

11b1(rgyud): ma de la skyabs su 'dod par gyis//bsam gyis mi khyab stobs dang smon lam rnams dang ldan//thabs dang byang chub yan lag ting 'dzin rnams kyis dag/rnam par grol ba'i lam dang stobs bcud ldan pa yi//thub pa
